/* What is an Emotional Support Animal? */

/* An Emotional Support Animal (ESA) is a companion animal that provides comfort and emotional stability to individuals with mental or emotional health conditions. ESAs are not trained to perform specific tasks but offer therapeutic benefits simply through their presence. They can help reduce stress, ease feelings of loneliness, and improve overall emotional well-being. ESAs are recognized under certain housing laws, allowing individuals to live with them in places that might otherwise have pet restrictions. To qualify, a licensed mental health professional must provide a formal recommendation explaining how the animal supports your emotional health. For individuals who may require additional support, evaluations for Psychiatric Service Dogs (PSDs) are also available when clinically appropriate. */

/* You might consider an Emotional Support Animal if you face challenges that affect your emotional or mental well-being. Situations that could indicate a need for an ESA include: Feeling persistent anxiety, depression, or stress that impacts daily life. Difficulty coping with emotional challenges on your own Experiencing frequent loneliness or isolation Noticing that being around animals naturally brings you comfort or a sense of calm Struggling to maintain emotional balance during stressful situations It’s important to note that an ESA should only be recommended following a proper evaluation by a licensed mental health professional. Documentation obtained without professional guidance may not be valid or recognized under housing laws. */

/* Since 2019, My ESA Therapist has connected individuals with licensed mental health professionals for emotional support animal (ESA) evaluations. If an ESA is determined to be clinically appropriate following an independent assessment, providers may issue documentation to support housing accommodation requests under applicable federal housing laws. Our network includes state-licensed mental health professionals who conduct independent telehealth evaluations in accordance with recognized clinical standards. Available in eligible U.S. states, every evaluation is based solely on professional clinical judgment and aligns with Fair Housing Act and HUD guidance where applicable. */
